## Methodology

### Extraction Strategies

1. **Pattern Matching:** Identified capitalized sequences with geographic indicators
2. **Geographic Features:** Extracted explicit patterns like 'Name + Type' (e.g., Victoria Island)
3. **Section Analysis:** Targeted sections like 'Principal Towns', 'Geographical Features'
4. **Context Validation:** Verified toponyms using surrounding context

### Sources Scanned

- OCR results from `colonial-office-list-{year}/olmocr_results.md`
- Manual parsed files from `output_2/{year}_manual_parsed/`

### Quality Assurance

- Filtered common words and invalid patterns
- Deduplicated toponyms by name
- Added full provenance with source file, line number, and context
- Confidence scores based on extraction method

## Output Files

Enhanced knowledge graph files with toponyms:

- `knowledge_graph_extracts_v3/1961_extracted_toponyms.json`
- `knowledge_graph_extracts_v3/1962_extracted_toponyms.json`

- `knowledge_graph_extracts_v3/1966_extracted_toponyms.json`
